Description:
  vion — the VION platform from the command line. Every operation of the cloud API is a command; sign in 
  once, then call any of them.

Usage:
  vion [command] [options]

Options:
  --env <name>              The environment: production, test, or one defined with `context use`.
  --integrator <id>         The integrator id, filling {integratorId} for this call.
  --tenant <id>             The tenant id, filling {tenantId} for this call.
  --project <id>            The project id, filling projectId for this call.
  --platform <id>           The platform id, filling {platformId} for this call.
  --json                    Write the response as JSON, and nothing else, to standard output.
  --fields <a,b.c>          Keep only these members, comma-separated, dotted for a nested one.
  --yes                     Confirm an irreversible operation without asking.
  --dry-run                 Check the request without applying it, on an operation that offers it.
  --idempotency-key <key>   The Idempotency-Key to send on a write; a fresh one when absent.
  --if-match <version>      The version to send as If-Match on a versioned update.
  --token <->               Read the bearer token from standard input: `--token -`.
  --client-id <id>          A service account's client id, with --client-secret.
  --client-secret <secret>  A service account's client secret, with --client-id.
  --verbose                 Show the request and the response headers on standard error.
  -?, -h, --help            Show help and usage information
  --version                 Show version information

Commands:
  tenant               Operations of a tenant: its gateways, projects, dashboards, rules, users and more.
  integrator           Operations of an integrator: its tenants, gateways, libraries, templates and accounts.
  platform             Operations of the platform: integrators, platform accounts and observability.
  me                   The caller's own: identity, notifications, preferences, profile.
  ops                  List the operations of the committed document: the command, the operationId, the 
                       method and path, the effect and the write-safety flags.
  api <method> <path>  Send a raw request to the environment's API with the resolved token, and render the 
                       answer as any command's.

---

## vion tenant widget-definition create
Description:
  Create a new tenant-owned widget definition

Usage:
  vion tenant widget-definition create [options]

Options:
  --input <file|->             The JSON body from a file, or `-` for standard input.
  -f, --raw-field <key=value>  A body member as a string: key=value; a dotted key nests.
  -F, --field <key=value>      A body member typed as JSON: true, false, null, a number, @file for a file's 
                               content, else a string.
  -?, -h, --help               Show help and usage information
  --env <name>                 The environment: production, test, or one defined with `context use`.
  --integrator <id>            The integrator id, filling {integratorId} for this call.
  --tenant <id>                The tenant id, filling {tenantId} for this call.

Commands:
  get  The theme to render a tenant context with: per field, the tenant's value or else its owning 
       integrator's. Only fields actually set at those two levels come back — the deployment and VION layers 
       underneath are the client's, so a tenant with nothing set anywhere is a 200 with every field null. 
       This is the resolved document, not the tenant's stored overrides; the overrides are authoring data and 
       are read by the integrator that owns them, on the integrator route.

---
## vion tenant white-label-theme get
Description:
  The theme to render a tenant context with: per field, the tenant's value or else its owning integrator's. 
  Only fields actually set at those two levels come back — the deployment and VION layers underneath are the 
  client's, so a tenant with nothing set anywhere is a 200 with every field null. This is the resolved 
  document, not the tenant's stored overrides; the overrides are authoring data and are read by the 
  integrator that owns them, on the integrator route.

Usage:
  vion tenant white-label-theme get [options]

Options:
  -?, -h, --help            Show help and usage information
  --env <name>              The environment: production, test, or one defined with `context use`.
  --integrator <id>         The integrator id, filling {integratorId} for this call.
  --tenant <id>             The tenant id, filling {tenantId} for this call.
  --project <id>            The project id, filling projectId for this call.
  --platform <id>           The platform id, filling {platformId} for this call.
  --json                    Write the response as JSON, and nothing else, to standard output.
  --fields <a,b.c>          Keep only these members, comma-separated, dotted for a nested one.
  --yes                     Confirm an irreversible operation without asking.
  --dry-run                 Check the request without applying it, on an operation that offers it.
  --idempotency-key <key>   The Idempotency-Key to send on a write; a fresh one when absent.
  --if-match <version>      The version to send as If-Match on a versioned update.
  --token <->               Read the bearer token from standard input: `--token -`.
  --client-id <id>          A service account's client id, with --client-secret.
  --client-secret <secret>  A service account's client secret, with --client-id.
  --verbose                 Show the request and the response headers on standard error.

## vion tenant system-health list
Description:

Usage:
  vion tenant system-health list [options]

Options:
  --edge-gateway-id <uuid>  Lists only the rows of this edge gateway of the tenant. It narrows the rows the 
                            caller may read and never adds one, and an id that is no edge gateway of the 
                            tenant is a 400.

---
## vion tenant system-event list
Description:
  An event is in the project its gateway was in when it occurred, and on that gateway. The list ends where 
  `nextCursor` is null.

Usage:
  vion tenant system-event list [options]

Options:
  --edge-gateway-id <uuid>  Lists only the rows of this edge gateway of the tenant. It narrows the rows the 
                            caller may read and never adds one, and an id that is no edge gateway of the 
                            tenant is a 400.
  --from <iso-8601>         Lists only the events that occurred at or after this time.
  --to <iso-8601>           Lists only the events that occurred before this time, which must be later than 
                            from.
  --limit <n>               The largest number of events on the page, 100 when omitted. A value above the 
                            maximum of 500 returns 500 events and is not an error, and a value below 1 is.
  --cursor <value>          The `nextCursor` of the previous page, or none for the first page. A cursor is 
                            not bound to the filters it was read with, so sending it with other filters pages 
                            a list it was not issued for. An event that arrives late, with a time older than 
                            the last one a page listed, is not on the pages that follow it.
  -?, -h, --help            Show help and usage information
  --env <name>              The environment: production, test, or one defined with `context use`.
  --integrator <id>         The integrator id, filling {integratorId} for this call.
  --tenant <id>             The tenant id, filling {tenantId} for this call.
  --project <id>            The project id, filling projectId for this call.
  --platform <id>           The platform id, filling {platformId} for this call.
  --json                    Write the response as JSON, and nothing else, to standard output.
  --fields <a,b.c>          Keep only these members, comma-separated, dotted for a nested one.
  --yes                     Confirm an irreversible operation without asking.
  --dry-run                 Check the request without applying it, on an operation that offers it.
  --idempotency-key <key>   The Idempotency-Key to send on a write; a fresh one when absent.
  --if-match <version>      The version to send as If-Match on a versioned update.
  --token <->               Read the bearer token from standard input: `--token -`.
  --client-id <id>          A service account's client id, with --client-secret.
  --client-secret <secret>  A service account's client secret, with --client-id.
  --verbose                 Show the request and the response headers on standard error.

Usage:
  vion tenant support-grant [command] [options]

Options:
  -?, -h, --help            Show help and usage information
  --env <name>              The environment: production, test, or one defined with `context use`.
  --integrator <id>         The integrator id, filling {integratorId} for this call.
  --tenant <id>             The tenant id, filling {tenantId} for this call.
  --project <id>            The project id, filling projectId for this call.
  --platform <id>           The platform id, filling {platformId} for this call.
  --json                    Write the response as JSON, and nothing else, to standard output.
  --fields <a,b.c>          Keep only these members, comma-separated, dotted for a nested one.
  --yes                     Confirm an irreversible operation without asking.
  --dry-run                 Check the request without applying it, on an operation that offers it.
  --idempotency-key <key>   The Idempotency-Key to send on a write; a fresh one when absent.
  --if-match <version>      The version to send as If-Match on a versioned update.
  --token <->               Read the bearer token from standard input: `--token -`.
  --client-id <id>          A service account's client id, with --client-secret.
  --client-secret <secret>  A service account's client secret, with --client-id.
  --verbose                 Show the request and the response headers on standard error.

Commands:
  list     A holder of `EdgeGateway.ManageSupportAccess` at the root sees every grant. Anyone else sees the 
           grants covering a gateway they read, and the grants at a scope where they hold it.
  create   "Invite support": create consent directly, with no pending request behind it. Sessions of the 
           granted kinds then start without entering the inbox at all.
  approve  Approve a pending request on the terms the tenant chooses. One grant per checked grantee kind, 
           each independently listed and revocable; the grantee then opens its own sessions under it.
  deny     Deny a pending request. Nothing was admitted under it, so the refusal is the whole effect.
  revoke   Withdraw consent. New sessions of that kind stop immediately and the reaper terminates the ones 
           already riding this grant.

---

## vion tenant service-property
Description:
  The service property operations.

  vion tenant service-property [command] [options]

Options:
  -?, -h, --help            Show help and usage information
  --env <name>              The environment: production, test, or one defined with `context use`.
  --integrator <id>         The integrator id, filling {integratorId} for this call.
  --tenant <id>             The tenant id, filling {tenantId} for this call.
  --project <id>            The project id, filling projectId for this call.
  --platform <id>           The platform id, filling {platformId} for this call.
  --json                    Write the response as JSON, and nothing else, to standard output.
  --fields <a,b.c>          Keep only these members, comma-separated, dotted for a nested one.
  --yes                     Confirm an irreversible operation without asking.
  --dry-run                 Check the request without applying it, on an operation that offers it.
  --idempotency-key <key>   The Idempotency-Key to send on a write; a fresh one when absent.
  --if-match <version>      The version to send as If-Match on a versioned update.
  --token <->               Read the bearer token from standard input: `--token -`.
  --client-id <id>          A service account's client id, with --client-secret.
  --client-secret <secret>  A service account's client secret, with --client-id.
  --verbose                 Show the request and the response headers on standard error.

Commands:
  subscribe  Start notification of property value changes. The actual property values are sent via MQTT to 
             the topic provided in the configuration of the service property. The flow is: call this endpoint 
             with the properties to watch and a subscriberId, then connect to the MQTT broker with the same 
             bearer token and read the values from the topics this call returns. subscriberId is not 
             free-form: it must equal the MQTT client id used on that connection, 
             {identityProviderUserId}_{exactly 6 alphanumeric characters}_, trailing underscore included, and 
             the subscription must be made at QoS 0. RabbitMQ enforces both and fails silently on violation: 
             CONNECT succeeds, SUBSCRIBE is refused, the connection closes, nothing names a reason. The 
             subscription is kept alive by the MQTT connection, so the client must register the last will 
             topic cloud/subscriber/lastWill/{identityProviderUserId}/{subscriberId} - the cloud tears the 
             subscription down when that last will fires. Worked examples: 
             https://docs.vion.swiss/cloud-api/minimal-client
  set        Set the value of a service property.

---
## vion tenant service-property subscribe
Description:
  Start notification of property value changes. The actual property values are sent via MQTT to the topic 
  provided in the configuration of the service property. The flow is: call this endpoint with the properties 
  to watch and a subscriberId, then connect to the MQTT broker with the same bearer token and read the values 
  from the topics this call returns. subscriberId is not free-form: it must equal the MQTT client id used on 
  that connection, {identityProviderUserId}_{exactly 6 alphanumeric characters}_, trailing underscore 
  included, and the subscription must be made at QoS 0. RabbitMQ enforces both and fails silently on 
  violation: CONNECT succeeds, SUBSCRIBE is refused, the connection closes, nothing names a reason. The 
  subscription is kept alive by the MQTT connection, so the client must register the last will topic 
  cloud/subscriber/lastWill/{identityProviderUserId}/{subscriberId} - the cloud tears the subscription down 
  when that last will fires. Worked examples: https://docs.vion.swiss/cloud-api/minimal-client

Usage:
  vion tenant service-property subscribe [options]

Options:
  --input <file|->             The JSON body from a file, or `-` for standard input.
  -f, --raw-field <key=value>  A body member as a string: key=value; a dotted key nests.
  -F, --field <key=value>      A body member typed as JSON: true, false, null, a number, @file for a file's 
                               content, else a string.
  -?, -h, --help               Show help and usage information
  --env <name>                 The environment: production, test, or one defined with `context use`.
  --integrator <id>            The integrator id, filling {integratorId} for this call.
  --tenant <id>                The tenant id, filling {tenantId} for this call.
  --project <id>               The project id, filling projectId for this call.
  --platform <id>              The platform id, filling {platformId} for this call.
  --json                       Write the response as JSON, and nothing else, to standard output.
  --fields <a,b.c>             Keep only these members, comma-separated, dotted for a nested one.
  --yes                        Confirm an irreversible operation without asking.
  --dry-run                    Check the request without applying it, on an operation that offers it.
  --idempotency-key <key>      The Idempotency-Key to send on a write; a fresh one when absent.
  --if-match <version>         The version to send as If-Match on a versioned update.
  --token <->                  Read the bearer token from standard input: `--token -`.
  --client-id <id>             A service account's client id, with --client-secret.
  --client-secret <secret>     A service account's client secret, with --client-id.
  --verbose                    Show the request and the response headers on standard error.

       against the web-SSH bridge, for the tenant user who opened the session. Carries NO credential — the 
       short-lived SSH certificate is minted and held by the bridge; the user authenticates over WSS with 
       their own VION identity, and acknowledges the recording banner to the bridge before it opens the PTY.

## vion tenant console-connection get

  and acknowledges the recording banner to the bridge before it opens the PTY.

## vion tenant overview-composition
Description:
  The overview composition operations.

Usage:
  vion tenant overview-composition [command] [options]

Options:
  -?, -h, --help            Show help and usage information
  --env <name>              The environment: production, test, or one defined with `context use`.
  --integrator <id>         The integrator id, filling {integratorId} for this call.
  --tenant <id>             The tenant id, filling {tenantId} for this call.
  --project <id>            The project id, filling projectId for this call.
  --platform <id>           The platform id, filling {platformId} for this call.
  --json                    Write the response as JSON, and nothing else, to standard output.
  --fields <a,b.c>          Keep only these members, comma-separated, dotted for a nested one.
  --yes                     Confirm an irreversible operation without asking.
  --dry-run                 Check the request without applying it, on an operation that offers it.
  --idempotency-key <key>   The Idempotency-Key to send on a write; a fresh one when absent.
  --if-match <version>      The version to send as If-Match on a versioned update.
  --token <->               Read the bearer token from standard input: `--token -`.
  --client-id <id>          A service account's client id, with --client-secret.
  --client-secret <secret>  A service account's client secret, with --client-id.
  --verbose                 Show the request and the response headers on standard error.

Commands:
  get     The composition to render this tenant's overview page with: per section, the tenant's own if it 
          authored one, else its integrator's row for this tenant, else its integrator's default. A tenant 
          whose integrator has composed nothing is a 200 with two empty sections, not a 404. origins says 
          which layer supplied each section and own is the tenant's stored row, so an authoring form can tell 
          "I set this" from "I inherited this" without a second request. It cannot see further: an inherited 
          section looks the same whether the integrator aimed it at this tenant or ships it to all of them.
  upsert  Replaces the tenant's own composition layer. A full upsert: a section left null is cleared back to 
          inherited, and a section set to an empty list is deliberately empty and hides whatever it inherits. 
          Answers with the resolved document, so the caller sees what a cleared section fell back to.
  delete  Drops the tenant's own layer entirely, so every section falls back to what it inherits. Resetting a 
          tenant that has authored nothing is a success.

---
## vion tenant overview-composition get

## vion tenant overview-composition upsert
Description:
  Replaces the tenant's own composition layer. A full upsert: a section left null is cleared back to 
  inherited, and a section set to an empty list is deliberately empty and hides whatever it inherits. Answers 
  with the resolved document, so the caller sees what a cleared section fell back to.

Usage:
  vion tenant overview-composition upsert [options]

Options:
  --input <file|->             The JSON body from a file, or `-` for standard input.
  -f, --raw-field <key=value>  A body member as a string: key=value; a dotted key nests.

## vion tenant measuring-point-series list
Description:
  Retrieves measured and calculated data for measuring points.

Usage:
  vion tenant measuring-point-series list [options]

Options:
  --from <iso-8601>                             The time the series starts at, the values measured at or 
                                                after it included.
  --to <iso-8601>                               The time the series ends at, the values measured before it 
                                                included, which must be later than `from`.
  --aggregation-interval <value>                The interval in which the data should be aggregated. One of: 
                                                OneMinute, FiveMinutes, FifteenMinutes, Hourly, Daily, 
                                                Monthly, Yearly.
  --edge-gateway-ids <value>                    A list of GUIDs, each uniquely identifying an edge gateway.
  --service-provider-identifiers <value>        A list of service provider identifiers to filter by.
  --service-identifiers <value>                 A list of service identifiers to filter by.
  --measuring-point-identifiers <value>         A list of human-readable identifiers unique within the service
  --measuring-point-unique-identifiers <value>  A list of unique measuring point identifiers
  --filling-mode <value>                        Specifies whether buckets without measurements are filled, 
                                                and how. None, the default, leaves them out. Linear draws a 
                                                line between the neighbouring buckets; Stepwise carries the 
                                                last value forward. Buckets before the first value are 
                                                omitted, as are buckets after the last one under Linear and 
                                                after now under Stepwise. A filled bucket sits on the same 
                                                grid as the measured ones and carries Interpolated quality 
                                                and no extremes. A filled series holds at most 20000 buckets; 
                                                a longer window is refused with a 400. One of: None, Linear, 
                                                Stepwise.
  --include-extremes                            Specifies whether each bucket's minimum and maximum value 
                                                should be returned alongside its average.
  -?, -h, --help                                Show help and usage information
  --env <name>                                  The environment: production, test, or one defined with 
                                                `context use`.
  --integrator <id>                             The integrator id, filling {integratorId} for this call.
  --tenant <id>                                 The tenant id, filling {tenantId} for this call.
  --project <id>                                The project id, filling projectId for this call.
  --platform <id>                               The platform id, filling {platformId} for this call.
  --json                                        Write the response as JSON, and nothing else, to standard 
                                                output.
  --fields <a,b.c>                              Keep only these members, comma-separated, dotted for a nested 
                                                one.
  --yes                                         Confirm an irreversible operation without asking.
  --dry-run                                     Check the request without applying it, on an operation that 
                                                offers it.
  --idempotency-key <key>                       The Idempotency-Key to send on a write; a fresh one when 
                                                absent.
  --if-match <version>                          The version to send as If-Match on a versioned update.
  --token <->                                   Read the bearer token from standard input: `--token -`.
  --client-id <id>                              A service account's client id, with --client-secret.
  --client-secret <secret>                      A service account's client secret, with --client-id.
  --verbose                                     Show the request and the response headers on standard error.

  The logic configuration operations.

  vion tenant logic-configuration [command] [options]

Options:
  -?, -h, --help            Show help and usage information
  --env <name>              The environment: production, test, or one defined with `context use`.
  --integrator <id>         The integrator id, filling {integratorId} for this call.
  --tenant <id>             The tenant id, filling {tenantId} for this call.
  --project <id>            The project id, filling projectId for this call.
  --platform <id>           The platform id, filling {platformId} for this call.
  --json                    Write the response as JSON, and nothing else, to standard output.
  --fields <a,b.c>          Keep only these members, comma-separated, dotted for a nested one.
  --yes                     Confirm an irreversible operation without asking.
  --dry-run                 Check the request without applying it, on an operation that offers it.
  --idempotency-key <key>   The Idempotency-Key to send on a write; a fresh one when absent.
  --if-match <version>      The version to send as If-Match on a versioned update.

Commands:
  list      Get all logic configurations for a tenant, maybe add filters, e.g. by edge gateway
  create    Create a new logic configuration for a tenant
  update    Update an existing logic configuration for a tenant. While the configuration is the active one 
            its content is frozen and a change to it fails with 409; the configuration's own name, 
            description and editor layout stay editable. A logic block instance's name is content, so 
            renaming one does not.
  delete    Delete a logic configuration for a tenant. Fails with 409 if the configuration is in use — either 
            the active one, or still deployed on an edge gateway.
  activate  Activate a logic configuration for a tenant. All edge gateways are updated if there are changes 
            to the currently active configuration.

## vion tenant logic-block-translation-bundle get

  The tenant's translation bundle for one locale: a flat `{ key: value }` map, under `translations`, merged 
  across every library its parent integrator may consume (own ∪ public ∪ granted). Exact-locale rows only — a 
  key the bundle does not carry renders from the compiled source string on the client.

Options:

  -?, -h, --help            Show help and usage information

## vion tenant logic-block-definition
Description:
  The logic block definition operations.

Usage:
  vion tenant logic-block-definition [command] [options]

Options:
  -?, -h, --help            Show help and usage information
  --env <name>              The environment: production, test, or one defined with `context use`.
  --integrator <id>         The integrator id, filling {integratorId} for this call.
  --tenant <id>             The tenant id, filling {tenantId} for this call.
  --project <id>            The project id, filling projectId for this call.
  --platform <id>           The platform id, filling {platformId} for this call.
  --json                    Write the response as JSON, and nothing else, to standard output.
  --fields <a,b.c>          Keep only these members, comma-separated, dotted for a nested one.
  --yes                     Confirm an irreversible operation without asking.
  --dry-run                 Check the request without applying it, on an operation that offers it.
  --idempotency-key <key>   The Idempotency-Key to send on a write; a fresh one when absent.
  --if-match <version>      The version to send as If-Match on a versioned update.
  --token <->               Read the bearer token from standard input: `--token -`.
  --client-id <id>          A service account's client id, with --client-secret.
  --client-secret <secret>  A service account's client secret, with --client-id.
  --verbose                 Show the request and the response headers on standard error.

Commands:
  list  Get all logic block definitions a tenant can use (own ∪ public ∪ granted, by its parent integrator).

## vion tenant alert-rule list
Description:
  Get all AlertRules for a tenant

Usage:
  vion tenant alert-rule list [options]

Options:

                            caller may read and never adds one, and an id that is no edge gateway of the 
                            tenant is a 400.

  get  Self-service: any authenticated tenant member, user or service account. Without `scopeId`, the scopes 
       are those where the caller or one of its groups holds an assignment of its own; a scope not listed has 
       the permissions of its nearest listed ancestor, or the root's. A `scopeId` that is not part of the 
       tenant answers with no scope type and no permissions.

---
## vion tenant my-permissions get
Description:
  Self-service: any authenticated tenant member, user or service account. Without `scopeId`, the scopes are 
  those where the caller or one of its groups holds an assignment of its own; a scope not listed has the 
  permissions of its nearest listed ancestor, or the root's. A `scopeId` that is not part of the tenant 
  answers with no scope type and no permissions.

Usage:
  vion tenant my-permissions get [options]

Options:
  --scope-id <value>        The scopes to resolve, the parameter repeated once per scope; none for the 
                            caller's own.
  -?, -h, --help            Show help and usage information
  --env <name>              The environment: production, test, or one defined with `context use`.
  --integrator <id>         The integrator id, filling {integratorId} for this call.
  --tenant <id>             The tenant id, filling {tenantId} for this call.
  --project <id>            The project id, filling projectId for this call.
  --platform <id>           The platform id, filling {platformId} for this call.
  --json                    Write the response as JSON, and nothing else, to standard output.
  --fields <a,b.c>          Keep only these members, comma-separated, dotted for a nested one.
  --yes                     Confirm an irreversible operation without asking.
  --dry-run                 Check the request without applying it, on an operation that offers it.
  --idempotency-key <key>   The Idempotency-Key to send on a write; a fresh one when absent.
  --if-match <version>      The version to send as If-Match on a versioned update.
  --token <->               Read the bearer token from standard input: `--token -`.
  --client-id <id>          A service account's client id, with --client-secret.

---
## vion tenant alert list
Description:
  An alert is in the project its gateway was in when it was raised, and on that gateway. The list ends where 
  `nextCursor` is null.

Usage:
  vion tenant alert list [options]

Options:
  --edge-gateway-id <uuid>  Lists only the rows of this edge gateway of the tenant. It narrows the rows the 
                            caller may read and never adds one, and an id that is no edge gateway of the 
                            tenant is a 400.
  --from <iso-8601>         Lists only the alerts raised at or after this time.
  --to <iso-8601>           Lists only the alerts raised before this time, which must be later than from.
  --status <value>          Lists only the active alerts, those not resolved, or only the resolved ones. One 
                            of: Active, Resolved.
  --limit <n>               The largest number of alerts on the page, 100 when omitted. A value above the 
                            maximum of 500 returns 500 alerts and is not an error, and a value below 1 is.
  --cursor <value>          The `nextCursor` of the previous page, or none for the first page. A cursor is 
                            not bound to the filters it was read with, so sending it with other filters pages 
                            a list it was not issued for.
  -?, -h, --help            Show help and usage information
  --env <name>              The environment: production, test, or one defined with `context use`.
  --integrator <id>         The integrator id, filling {integratorId} for this call.
  --tenant <id>             The tenant id, filling {tenantId} for this call.
  --project <id>            The project id, filling projectId for this call.
  --platform <id>           The platform id, filling {platformId} for this call.
  --json                    Write the response as JSON, and nothing else, to standard output.
  --fields <a,b.c>          Keep only these members, comma-separated, dotted for a nested one.
  --yes                     Confirm an irreversible operation without asking.
  --dry-run                 Check the request without applying it, on an operation that offers it.
  --idempotency-key <key>   The Idempotency-Key to send on a write; a fresh one when absent.
  --if-match <version>      The version to send as If-Match on a versioned update.
  --token <->               Read the bearer token from standard input: `--token -`.
  --client-id <id>          A service account's client id, with --client-secret.
  --client-secret <secret>  A service account's client secret, with --client-id.
  --verbose                 Show the request and the response headers on standard error.

  --idempotency-key <key>      The Idempotency-Key to send on a write; a fresh one when absent.
  --if-match <version>         The version to send as If-Match on a versioned update.
  --token <->                  Read the bearer token from standard input: `--token -`.
  --client-id <id>             A service account's client id, with --client-secret.
  --client-secret <secret>     A service account's client secret, with --client-id.
  --verbose                    Show the request and the response headers on standard error.

---
## vion integrator tenant-white-label-theme
Description:
  The tenant white label theme operations.

Usage:
  vion integrator tenant-white-label-theme [command] [options]

Options:
  -?, -h, --help            Show help and usage information
  --env <name>              The environment: production, test, or one defined with `context use`.
  --integrator <id>         The integrator id, filling {integratorId} for this call.
  --tenant <id>             The tenant id, filling {tenantId} for this call.
  --project <id>            The project id, filling projectId for this call.
  --platform <id>           The platform id, filling {platformId} for this call.
  --json                    Write the response as JSON, and nothing else, to standard output.
  --fields <a,b.c>          Keep only these members, comma-separated, dotted for a nested one.
  --yes                     Confirm an irreversible operation without asking.
  --dry-run                 Check the request without applying it, on an operation that offers it.
  --idempotency-key <key>   The Idempotency-Key to send on a write; a fresh one when absent.
  --if-match <version>      The version to send as If-Match on a versioned update.
  --token <->               Read the bearer token from standard input: `--token -`.
  --client-id <id>          A service account's client id, with --client-secret.
  --client-secret <secret>  A service account's client secret, with --client-id.
  --verbose                 Show the request and the response headers on standard error.

Commands:
  list    Every owned tenant's theme exactly as stored — the overrides that tenant sets, not the merged 
          result its members render. A tenant with no overrides is absent from the list rather than present 
          and all null: absence is what "inherits everything" means, and it keeps the response to the tenants 
          that have actually been themed.
  upsert  Replaces one tenant's overrides. A full upsert: a field left null is cleared back to inheriting the 
          integrator's value, and an all-null body takes the tenant level out of the fallback chain.

---

## vion integrator white-label-theme
Description:
  The white label theme operations.

Usage:
  vion integrator white-label-theme [command] [options]

Options:
  -?, -h, --help            Show help and usage information
  --env <name>              The environment: production, test, or one defined with `context use`.
  --integrator <id>         The integrator id, filling {integratorId} for this call.
  --tenant <id>             The tenant id, filling {tenantId} for this call.
  --project <id>            The project id, filling projectId for this call.
  --platform <id>           The platform id, filling {platformId} for this call.
  --json                    Write the response as JSON, and nothing else, to standard output.
  --fields <a,b.c>          Keep only these members, comma-separated, dotted for a nested one.
  --yes                     Confirm an irreversible operation without asking.
  --dry-run                 Check the request without applying it, on an operation that offers it.
  --idempotency-key <key>   The Idempotency-Key to send on a write; a fresh one when absent.
  --if-match <version>      The version to send as If-Match on a versioned update.

  --client-id <id>          A service account's client id, with --client-secret.
  --client-secret <secret>  A service account's client secret, with --client-id.
  --verbose                 Show the request and the response headers on standard error.

Commands:
  get     The integrator's theme: the fields it sets, and nothing else — the server never fills a deployment 
          or VION default, the client layers those underneath. Serves both the renderer and the authoring 
          form, because at this level "as stored" and "as resolved" are the same document. An integrator that 
          has never been themed is a 200 with every field null, not a 404.
  upsert  Replaces the integrator's theme. A full upsert: a field left null is cleared back to inherited, and 
          an all-null body takes the integrator level out of the fallback chain.

  --tenant <id>             The tenant id, filling {tenantId} for this call.
  --project <id>            The project id, filling projectId for this call.
  --platform <id>           The platform id, filling {platformId} for this call.
  --json                    Write the response as JSON, and nothing else, to standard output.
  --fields <a,b.c>          Keep only these members, comma-separated, dotted for a nested one.
  --yes                     Confirm an irreversible operation without asking.
  --dry-run                 Check the request without applying it, on an operation that offers it.
  --idempotency-key <key>   The Idempotency-Key to send on a write; a fresh one when absent.
  --if-match <version>      The version to send as If-Match on a versioned update.
  --token <->               Read the bearer token from standard input: `--token -`.
  --client-id <id>          A service account's client id, with --client-secret.
  --client-secret <secret>  A service account's client secret, with --client-id.
  --verbose                 Show the request and the response headers on standard error.

Commands:
  create  The machine-identity form of the owner invitation beside this: no email user, no token, no accept 
          step — the redeem is the client_credentials grant. The created account does NOT appear in the 
          tenant's owners list, which enumerates invited users; it appears in the tenant's own Access 
          console, which is membership-driven.
  list    Get the service accounts that own the tenant. Never returns a secret.
  delete  A service account's identity and its membership are one row, so revoking ownership means destroying 
          the account — the honest mirror of removing an owner user.

---

          with two empty sections, not a 404.
  upsert  Replaces the integrator\'s composition. A full upsert: a section left null is cleared back to 
          inherited, and a body with both sections empty stores an empty overview rather than deleting the 
          row.

## vion integrator tenant-overview-composition-layer
Description:
  The tenant overview composition layer operations.

Usage:
  vion integrator tenant-overview-composition-layer [command] [options]

Options:
  -?, -h, --help            Show help and usage information
  --env <name>              The environment: production, test, or one defined with `context use`.
  --integrator <id>         The integrator id, filling {integratorId} for this call.
  --tenant <id>             The tenant id, filling {tenantId} for this call.
  --project <id>            The project id, filling projectId for this call.
  --platform <id>           The platform id, filling {platformId} for this call.
  --json                    Write the response as JSON, and nothing else, to standard output.
  --fields <a,b.c>          Keep only these members, comma-separated, dotted for a nested one.
  --yes                     Confirm an irreversible operation without asking.
  --dry-run                 Check the request without applying it, on an operation that offers it.
  --idempotency-key <key>   The Idempotency-Key to send on a write; a fresh one when absent.
  --if-match <version>      The version to send as If-Match on a versioned update.
  --token <->               Read the bearer token from standard input: `--token -`.
  --client-id <id>          A service account's client id, with --client-secret.
  --client-secret <secret>  A service account's client secret, with --client-id.
  --verbose                 Show the request and the response headers on standard error.

Commands:
  list    Every tenant-scoped layer of every owned tenant, exactly as stored — what each level authored, not 
          the merged document its members render. A tenant nobody has composed for is absent from the list 
          rather than present and all null: absence is what "inherits everything" means.
  upsert  Replaces the layer this integrator authored for one tenant. A full upsert: a section left null is 
          cleared back to the integrator's default, and an empty list is a deliberate empty section that the 
          tenant sees as inherited and empty.
  delete  Drops the layer this integrator authored for one tenant, so it falls back to the integrator 
          default. Resetting a tenant that has no such layer is a success.

---

Commands:
  deploy  Deploy Software Release to Edge Gateways
  list    Available software releases (the gateway-independent release catalog). Each entry is a single 
          active Mender release enriched with its parsed software name and version, an `IsLatest` flag 
          (newest version for its software name), and the device types it is compatible with. The list is not 
          filtered by the integrator's existing gateways so that software profiles can pin and upgrade 
          releases for hardware that has not been onboarded yet; callers filter by device type client-side.

---
## vion integrator software-release deploy
Description:
  Deploy Software Release to Edge Gateways

Usage:
  vion integrator software-release deploy [options]

Options:
  --input <file|->             The JSON body from a file, or `-` for standard input.
  -f, --raw-field <key=value>  A body member as a string: key=value; a dotted key nests.
  -F, --field <key=value>      A body member typed as JSON: true, false, null, a number, @file for a file's 
                               content, else a string.
  -?, -h, --help               Show help and usage information
  --env <name>                 The environment: production, test, or one defined with `context use`.
  --integrator <id>            The integrator id, filling {integratorId} for this call.
  --tenant <id>                The tenant id, filling {tenantId} for this call.
  --project <id>               The project id, filling projectId for this call.
  --platform <id>              The platform id, filling {platformId} for this call.
  --json                       Write the response as JSON, and nothing else, to standard output.
  --fields <a,b.c>             Keep only these members, comma-separated, dotted for a nested one.
  --yes                        Confirm an irreversible operation without asking.
  --dry-run                    Check the request without applying it, on an operation that offers it.
  --idempotency-key <key>      The Idempotency-Key to send on a write; a fresh one when absent.
  --if-match <version>         The version to send as If-Match on a versioned update.
  --token <->                  Read the bearer token from standard input: `--token -`.
  --client-id <id>             A service account's client id, with --client-secret.
  --client-secret <secret>     A service account's client secret, with --client-id.
  --verbose                    Show the request and the response headers on standard error.

---
## vion integrator software-release list
Description:
  Available software releases (the gateway-independent release catalog). Each entry is a single active Mender 
  release enriched with its parsed software name and version, an `IsLatest` flag (newest version for its 
  software name), and the device types it is compatible with. The list is not filtered by the integrator's 
  existing gateways so that software profiles can pin and upgrade releases for hardware that has not been 
  onboarded yet; callers filter by device type client-side.

Usage:
  vion integrator software-release list [options]

Options:
  -?, -h, --help            Show help and usage information
  --env <name>              The environment: production, test, or one defined with `context use`.
  --integrator <id>         The integrator id, filling {integratorId} for this call.
  --tenant <id>             The tenant id, filling {tenantId} for this call.
  --project <id>            The project id, filling projectId for this call.
  --platform <id>           The platform id, filling {platformId} for this call.
  --json                    Write the response as JSON, and nothing else, to standard output.
  --fields <a,b.c>          Keep only these members, comma-separated, dotted for a nested one.
  --yes                     Confirm an irreversible operation without asking.
  --dry-run                 Check the request without applying it, on an operation that offers it.
  --idempotency-key <key>   The Idempotency-Key to send on a write; a fresh one when absent.
  --if-match <version>      The version to send as If-Match on a versioned update.
  --token <->               Read the bearer token from standard input: `--token -`.
  --client-id <id>          A service account's client id, with --client-secret.
  --client-secret <secret>  A service account's client secret, with --client-id.
  --verbose                 Show the request and the response headers on standard error.

---
## vion integrator software-deployment
Description:
  The software deployment operations.

Usage:
  vion integrator software-deployment [command] [options]

Options:
  -?, -h, --help            Show help and usage information
  --env <name>              The environment: production, test, or one defined with `context use`.
  --integrator <id>         The integrator id, filling {integratorId} for this call.
  --tenant <id>             The tenant id, filling {tenantId} for this call.
  --project <id>            The project id, filling projectId for this call.
  --platform <id>           The platform id, filling {platformId} for this call.
  --json                    Write the response as JSON, and nothing else, to standard output.
  --fields <a,b.c>          Keep only these members, comma-separated, dotted for a nested one.
  --yes                     Confirm an irreversible operation without asking.
  --dry-run                 Check the request without applying it, on an operation that offers it.
  --idempotency-key <key>   The Idempotency-Key to send on a write; a fresh one when absent.
  --if-match <version>      The version to send as If-Match on a versioned update.

  --client-id <id>          A service account's client id, with --client-secret.
  --client-secret <secret>  A service account's client secret, with --client-id.
  --verbose                 Show the request and the response headers on standard error.

Commands:
  list  Get all deployments for an integrator, grouped by software release. This will return the latest 
        deployment for each software release and the edge gateways it is deployed to.

## vion integrator edge-gateway
Description:
  The edge gateway operations.

Usage:
  vion integrator edge-gateway [command] [options]

Options:
  -?, -h, --help            Show help and usage information
  --env <name>              The environment: production, test, or one defined with `context use`.
  --integrator <id>         The integrator id, filling {integratorId} for this call.
  --tenant <id>             The tenant id, filling {tenantId} for this call.
  --project <id>            The project id, filling projectId for this call.
  --platform <id>           The platform id, filling {platformId} for this call.
  --json                    Write the response as JSON, and nothing else, to standard output.
  --fields <a,b.c>          Keep only these members, comma-separated, dotted for a nested one.
  --yes                     Confirm an irreversible operation without asking.
  --dry-run                 Check the request without applying it, on an operation that offers it.
  --idempotency-key <key>   The Idempotency-Key to send on a write; a fresh one when absent.
  --if-match <version>      The version to send as If-Match on a versioned update.
  --token <->               Read the bearer token from standard input: `--token -`.
  --client-id <id>          A service account's client id, with --client-secret.
  --client-secret <secret>  A service account's client secret, with --client-id.
  --verbose                 Show the request and the response headers on standard error.

Commands:
  restart       Restarts a single component on one of the integrator's edge gateways. Mirrors the tenant 
                `POST /Tenant/{tenantId}/Restarts/{id}/{component}`, but is authorized at the integrator root 
                instead of at the gateway's project.
  list          Returns all edge gateways for an integrator
  create        Creates an edge gateway for an integrator
  update        Allows to assign an edge gateway to a tenant
  delete        Deletes an edge gateway
  recommission  Re-commissions an existing edge gateway (preserves the gateway id).

---
## vion integrator edge-gateway restart
Description:
  Restarts a single component on one of the integrator's edge gateways. Mirrors the tenant `POST 
  /Tenant/{tenantId}/Restarts/{id}/{component}`, but is authorized at the integrator root instead of at the 
  gateway's project.

Usage:
  vion integrator edge-gateway restart [options]

Options:
  --id <uuid>               The edge gateway's id.
  --component <value>       The component.
  -?, -h, --help            Show help and usage information
  --env <name>              The environment: production, test, or one defined with `context use`.
  --integrator <id>         The integrator id, filling {integratorId} for this call.
  --tenant <id>             The tenant id, filling {tenantId} for this call.
  --project <id>            The project id, filling projectId for this call.
  --platform <id>           The platform id, filling {platformId} for this call.
  --json                    Write the response as JSON, and nothing else, to standard output.
  --fields <a,b.c>          Keep only these members, comma-separated, dotted for a nested one.
  --yes                     Confirm an irreversible operation without asking.
  --dry-run                 Check the request without applying it, on an operation that offers it.
  --idempotency-key <key>   The Idempotency-Key to send on a write; a fresh one when absent.
  --if-match <version>      The version to send as If-Match on a versioned update.
  --token <->               Read the bearer token from standard input: `--token -`.
  --client-id <id>          A service account's client id, with --client-secret.
  --client-secret <secret>  A service account's client secret, with --client-id.
  --verbose                 Show the request and the response headers on standard error.

---

## vion integrator logic-configuration-template list
Description:
  Get all logic configuration templates for an integrator

Usage:
  vion integrator logic-configuration-template list [options]

Options:
  -?, -h, --help            Show help and usage information
  --env <name>              The environment: production, test, or one defined with `context use`.
  --integrator <id>         The integrator id, filling {integratorId} for this call.
  --tenant <id>             The tenant id, filling {tenantId} for this call.
  --project <id>            The project id, filling projectId for this call.
  --platform <id>           The platform id, filling {platformId} for this call.
  --json                    Write the response as JSON, and nothing else, to standard output.
  --fields <a,b.c>          Keep only these members, comma-separated, dotted for a nested one.
  --yes                     Confirm an irreversible operation without asking.
  --dry-run                 Check the request without applying it, on an operation that offers it.
  --idempotency-key <key>   The Idempotency-Key to send on a write; a fresh one when absent.
  --if-match <version>      The version to send as If-Match on a versioned update.
  --token <->               Read the bearer token from standard input: `--token -`.
  --client-id <id>          A service account's client id, with --client-secret.
  --client-secret <secret>  A service account's client secret, with --client-id.
  --verbose                 Show the request and the response headers on standard error.

---
## vion integrator logic-configuration-template create
Description:
  Create a new logic configuration template

Usage:
  vion integrator logic-configuration-template create [options]

Options:
  --input <file|->             The JSON body from a file, or `-` for standard input.
  -f, --raw-field <key=value>  A body member as a string: key=value; a dotted key nests.
  -F, --field <key=value>      A body member typed as JSON: true, false, null, a number, @file for a file's 
                               content, else a string.
  -?, -h, --help               Show help and usage information
  --env <name>                 The environment: production, test, or one defined with `context use`.
  --integrator <id>            The integrator id, filling {integratorId} for this call.
  --tenant <id>                The tenant id, filling {tenantId} for this call.
  --project <id>               The project id, filling projectId for this call.
  --platform <id>              The platform id, filling {platformId} for this call.
  --json                       Write the response as JSON, and nothing else, to standard output.
  --fields <a,b.c>             Keep only these members, comma-separated, dotted for a nested one.
  --yes                        Confirm an irreversible operation without asking.
  --dry-run                    Check the request without applying it, on an operation that offers it.
  --idempotency-key <key>      The Idempotency-Key to send on a write; a fresh one when absent.
  --if-match <version>         The version to send as If-Match on a versioned update.
  --token <->                  Read the bearer token from standard input: `--token -`.
  --client-id <id>             A service account's client id, with --client-secret.
  --client-secret <secret>     A service account's client secret, with --client-id.
  --verbose                    Show the request and the response headers on standard error.

---

## vion integrator logic-block-library-translation
Description:
  The logic block library translation operations.

Usage:
  vion integrator logic-block-library-translation [command] [options]

Options:
  -?, -h, --help            Show help and usage information
  --env <name>              The environment: production, test, or one defined with `context use`.
  --integrator <id>         The integrator id, filling {integratorId} for this call.
  --tenant <id>             The tenant id, filling {tenantId} for this call.
  --project <id>            The project id, filling projectId for this call.
  --platform <id>           The platform id, filling {platformId} for this call.
  --json                    Write the response as JSON, and nothing else, to standard output.
  --fields <a,b.c>          Keep only these members, comma-separated, dotted for a nested one.
  --yes                     Confirm an irreversible operation without asking.
  --dry-run                 Check the request without applying it, on an operation that offers it.
  --idempotency-key <key>   The Idempotency-Key to send on a write; a fresh one when absent.
  --if-match <version>      The version to send as If-Match on a versioned update.
  --token <->               Read the bearer token from standard input: `--token -`.
  --client-id <id>          A service account's client id, with --client-secret.
  --client-secret <secret>  A service account's client secret, with --client-id.
  --verbose                 Show the request and the response headers on standard error.

Commands:
  list    The authoring matrix: every catalog row of the library joined with its locale cells. Orphaned rows 
          are included and flagged — they stay visible so an integrator can copy a translation over after a 
          rename.
  upsert  Bulk upsert of matrix cells. Last-write-wins; every written cell re-pins the source digest.
  delete  Reset-to-default for the listed catalog keys. With `locale` omitted every locale of each key is 
          cleared. Bulk so the editor can clear a filtered selection — orphaned rows, AI suggestions — in one 
          request; the keys are listed explicitly rather than re-derived from a server-side filter, because 
          the editor's view and the server can be out of sync.
  export  Exports the library's catalog for one locale: columns `key, context, source, target, status, 
          outdated` , UTF-8 with BOM, semicolon-delimited (German-locale Excel is the audience).
  import  Imports a translated CSV into one locale. Partial-accept: valid rows are applied, invalid ones are 
          reported with a line number and a reason.

---
## vion integrator logic-block-library-translation list
Description:
  The authoring matrix: every catalog row of the library joined with its locale cells. Orphaned rows are 
  included and flagged — they stay visible so an integrator can copy a translation over after a rename.

Usage:

Options:

  -?, -h, --help                   Show help and usage information
  --env <name>                     The environment: production, test, or one defined with `context use`.
  --integrator <id>                The integrator id, filling {integratorId} for this call.
  --tenant <id>                    The tenant id, filling {tenantId} for this call.
  --project <id>                   The project id, filling projectId for this call.
  --platform <id>                  The platform id, filling {platformId} for this call.
  --json                           Write the response as JSON, and nothing else, to standard output.
  --fields <a,b.c>                 Keep only these members, comma-separated, dotted for a nested one.
  --yes                            Confirm an irreversible operation without asking.
  --dry-run                        Check the request without applying it, on an operation that offers it.
  --idempotency-key <key>          The Idempotency-Key to send on a write; a fresh one when absent.
  --if-match <version>             The version to send as If-Match on a versioned update.
  --token <->                      Read the bearer token from standard input: `--token -`.
  --client-id <id>                 A service account's client id, with --client-secret.
  --client-secret <secret>         A service account's client secret, with --client-id.
  --verbose                        Show the request and the response headers on standard error.

## vion integrator logic-block-library-translation export
Description:
  Exports the library's catalog for one locale: columns `key, context, source, target, status, outdated` , 
  UTF-8 with BOM, semicolon-delimited (German-locale Excel is the audience).

Usage:
  vion integrator logic-block-library-translation export [options]

Options:
  --logic-block-library-id <uuid>  The library whose catalog is exported.
  --locale <value>                 The language exported, a BCP 47 plain-language tag such as `de`.
  -?, -h, --help                   Show help and usage information

  --integrator <id>                The integrator id, filling {integratorId} for this call.

  --dry-run                        Check the request without applying it, on an operation that offers it.

  --if-match <version>             The version to send as If-Match on a versioned update.
  --token <->                      Read the bearer token from standard input: `--token -`.
  --client-id <id>                 A service account's client id, with --client-secret.

  --verbose                        Show the request and the response headers on standard error.

  --json                           Write the response as JSON, and nothing else, to standard output.
  --fields <a,b.c>                 Keep only these members, comma-separated, dotted for a nested one.
  --yes                            Confirm an irreversible operation without asking.
  --dry-run                        Check the request without applying it, on an operation that offers it.

## vion integrator logic-block-translation-bundle
Description:
  The logic block translation bundle operations.

Usage:
  vion integrator logic-block-translation-bundle [command] [options]

Options:
  -?, -h, --help            Show help and usage information
  --env <name>              The environment: production, test, or one defined with `context use`.
  --integrator <id>         The integrator id, filling {integratorId} for this call.
  --tenant <id>             The tenant id, filling {tenantId} for this call.
  --project <id>            The project id, filling projectId for this call.
  --platform <id>           The platform id, filling {platformId} for this call.
  --json                    Write the response as JSON, and nothing else, to standard output.
  --fields <a,b.c>          Keep only these members, comma-separated, dotted for a nested one.
  --yes                     Confirm an irreversible operation without asking.
  --dry-run                 Check the request without applying it, on an operation that offers it.
  --idempotency-key <key>   The Idempotency-Key to send on a write; a fresh one when absent.
  --if-match <version>      The version to send as If-Match on a versioned update.
  --token <->               Read the bearer token from standard input: `--token -`.
  --client-id <id>          A service account's client id, with --client-secret.
  --client-secret <secret>  A service account's client secret, with --client-id.
  --verbose                 Show the request and the response headers on standard error.

Commands:
  get  The integrator's translation bundle for one locale: a flat `{ key: value }` map, under `translations`, 
       merged across every library the integrator may consume (own ∪ public ∪ granted). Exact-locale rows 
       only — a key the bundle does not carry renders from the compiled source string on the client.

---
## vion integrator logic-block-translation-bundle get
Description:
  The integrator's translation bundle for one locale: a flat `{ key: value }` map, under `translations`, 
  merged across every library the integrator may consume (own ∪ public ∪ granted). Exact-locale rows only — a 
  key the bundle does not carry renders from the compiled source string on the client.

Usage:
  vion integrator logic-block-translation-bundle get [options]

Options:
  --locale <value>          The language of the bundle, a BCP 47 plain-language tag such as `de`.
  -?, -h, --help            Show help and usage information
  --env <name>              The environment: production, test, or one defined with `context use`.
  --integrator <id>         The integrator id, filling {integratorId} for this call.
  --tenant <id>             The tenant id, filling {tenantId} for this call.
  --project <id>            The project id, filling projectId for this call.
  --platform <id>           The platform id, filling {platformId} for this call.
  --json                    Write the response as JSON, and nothing else, to standard output.
  --fields <a,b.c>          Keep only these members, comma-separated, dotted for a nested one.
  --yes                     Confirm an irreversible operation without asking.
  --dry-run                 Check the request without applying it, on an operation that offers it.
  --idempotency-key <key>   The Idempotency-Key to send on a write; a fresh one when absent.
  --if-match <version>      The version to send as If-Match on a versioned update.
  --token <->               Read the bearer token from standard input: `--token -`.
  --client-id <id>          A service account's client id, with --client-secret.
  --client-secret <secret>  A service account's client secret, with --client-id.
  --verbose                 Show the request and the response headers on standard error.

## vion integrator logic-block-library-grant

  vion integrator logic-block-library-grant [command] [options]

  -?, -h, --help            Show help and usage information

  --dry-run                 Check the request without applying it, on an operation that offers it.

  create  Grant one of the integrator's libraries to another integrator.
  delete  Revoke a grant. Prospective: running configurations keep running, but a configuration that still 
          references the now-invisible library fails its next activation until those blocks are removed.

Description:
  The effective roles a principal has across scopes, with provenance.

Usage:
  vion integrator principal-access list [options]

Options:
  --principal-id <uuid>     The principal's id.
  -?, -h, --help            Show help and usage information
  --env <name>              The environment: production, test, or one defined with `context use`.
  --integrator <id>         The integrator id, filling {integratorId} for this call.
  --tenant <id>             The tenant id, filling {tenantId} for this call.
  --project <id>            The project id, filling projectId for this call.
  --platform <id>           The platform id, filling {platformId} for this call.
  --json                    Write the response as JSON, and nothing else, to standard output.
  --fields <a,b.c>          Keep only these members, comma-separated, dotted for a nested one.
  --yes                     Confirm an irreversible operation without asking.
  --dry-run                 Check the request without applying it, on an operation that offers it.
  --idempotency-key <key>   The Idempotency-Key to send on a write; a fresh one when absent.
  --if-match <version>      The version to send as If-Match on a versioned update.
  --token <->               Read the bearer token from standard input: `--token -`.
  --client-id <id>          A service account's client id, with --client-secret.
  --client-secret <secret>  A service account's client secret, with --client-id.
  --verbose                 Show the request and the response headers on standard error.

## vion integrator my-permissions
Description:
  The my permissions operations.

Usage:
  vion integrator my-permissions [command] [options]

Options:
  -?, -h, --help            Show help and usage information
  --env <name>              The environment: production, test, or one defined with `context use`.
  --integrator <id>         The integrator id, filling {integratorId} for this call.
  --tenant <id>             The tenant id, filling {tenantId} for this call.
  --project <id>            The project id, filling projectId for this call.
  --platform <id>           The platform id, filling {platformId} for this call.
  --json                    Write the response as JSON, and nothing else, to standard output.
  --fields <a,b.c>          Keep only these members, comma-separated, dotted for a nested one.
  --yes                     Confirm an irreversible operation without asking.
  --dry-run                 Check the request without applying it, on an operation that offers it.
  --idempotency-key <key>   The Idempotency-Key to send on a write; a fresh one when absent.
  --if-match <version>      The version to send as If-Match on a versioned update.
  --token <->               Read the bearer token from standard input: `--token -`.
  --client-id <id>          A service account's client id, with --client-secret.
  --client-secret <secret>  A service account's client secret, with --client-id.
  --verbose                 Show the request and the response headers on standard error.

Commands:
  get  Self-service: any authenticated integrator member, user or service account. Without `scopeId`, the 
       scopes are those where the caller or one of its groups holds an assignment of its own; a scope not 
       listed has the permissions of its nearest listed ancestor, or the root's. A `scopeId` that is not part 
       of the integrator answers with no scope type and no permissions.

---
## vion integrator my-permissions get
Description:
  Self-service: any authenticated integrator member, user or service account. Without `scopeId`, the scopes 
  are those where the caller or one of its groups holds an assignment of its own; a scope not listed has the 
  permissions of its nearest listed ancestor, or the root's. A `scopeId` that is not part of the integrator 
  answers with no scope type and no permissions.

Usage:
  vion integrator my-permissions get [options]

Options:
  --scope-id <value>        The scopes to resolve, the parameter repeated once per scope; none for the 
                            caller's own.
  -?, -h, --help            Show help and usage information
  --env <name>              The environment: production, test, or one defined with `context use`.
  --integrator <id>         The integrator id, filling {integratorId} for this call.
  --tenant <id>             The tenant id, filling {tenantId} for this call.
  --project <id>            The project id, filling projectId for this call.
  --platform <id>           The platform id, filling {platformId} for this call.
  --json                    Write the response as JSON, and nothing else, to standard output.
  --fields <a,b.c>          Keep only these members, comma-separated, dotted for a nested one.
  --yes                     Confirm an irreversible operation without asking.
  --dry-run                 Check the request without applying it, on an operation that offers it.
  --idempotency-key <key>   The Idempotency-Key to send on a write; a fresh one when absent.
  --if-match <version>      The version to send as If-Match on a versioned update.
  --token <->               Read the bearer token from standard input: `--token -`.
  --client-id <id>          A service account's client id, with --client-secret.
  --client-secret <secret>  A service account's client secret, with --client-id.
  --verbose                 Show the request and the response headers on standard error.

## vion integrator invitation accept
Description:
  Accept an integrator invitation using the invitation token.

Usage:
  vion integrator invitation accept [options]

Options:
  --input <file|->             The JSON body from a file, or `-` for standard input.
  -f, --raw-field <key=value>  A body member as a string: key=value; a dotted key nests.
  -F, --field <key=value>      A body member typed as JSON: true, false, null, a number, @file for a file's 
                               content, else a string.
  -?, -h, --help               Show help and usage information
  --env <name>                 The environment: production, test, or one defined with `context use`.
  --integrator <id>            The integrator id, filling {integratorId} for this call.
  --tenant <id>                The tenant id, filling {tenantId} for this call.
  --project <id>               The project id, filling projectId for this call.
  --platform <id>              The platform id, filling {platformId} for this call.
  --json                       Write the response as JSON, and nothing else, to standard output.
  --fields <a,b.c>             Keep only these members, comma-separated, dotted for a nested one.
  --yes                        Confirm an irreversible operation without asking.
  --dry-run                    Check the request without applying it, on an operation that offers it.
  --idempotency-key <key>      The Idempotency-Key to send on a write; a fresh one when absent.
  --if-match <version>         The version to send as If-Match on a versioned update.
  --token <->                  Read the bearer token from standard input: `--token -`.
  --client-id <id>             A service account's client id, with --client-secret.
  --client-secret <secret>     A service account's client secret, with --client-id.
  --verbose                    Show the request and the response headers on standard error.

  vion integrator service-account update [options]

Options:
  --id <uuid>                  The service account's id.
  --input <file|->             The JSON body from a file, or `-` for standard input.
  -f, --raw-field <key=value>  A body member as a string: key=value; a dotted key nests.
  -F, --field <key=value>      A body member typed as JSON: true, false, null, a number, @file for a file's 
                               content, else a string.
  -?, -h, --help               Show help and usage information
  --env <name>                 The environment: production, test, or one defined with `context use`.
  --integrator <id>            The integrator id, filling {integratorId} for this call.
  --tenant <id>                The tenant id, filling {tenantId} for this call.
  --project <id>               The project id, filling projectId for this call.
  --platform <id>              The platform id, filling {platformId} for this call.
  --json                       Write the response as JSON, and nothing else, to standard output.
  --fields <a,b.c>             Keep only these members, comma-separated, dotted for a nested one.
  --yes                        Confirm an irreversible operation without asking.
  --dry-run                    Check the request without applying it, on an operation that offers it.
  --idempotency-key <key>      The Idempotency-Key to send on a write; a fresh one when absent.
  --if-match <version>         The version to send as If-Match on a versioned update.
  --token <->                  Read the bearer token from standard input: `--token -`.
  --client-id <id>             A service account's client id, with --client-secret.
  --client-secret <secret>     A service account's client secret, with --client-id.
  --verbose                    Show the request and the response headers on standard error.

  carries scope ids, names and types only, and no principal.

Usage:
  vion platform support-tenant-scope list [options]

Options:
  -?, -h, --help            Show help and usage information
  --env <name>              The environment: production, test, or one defined with `context use`.
  --integrator <id>         The integrator id, filling {integratorId} for this call.
  --tenant <id>             The tenant id, filling {tenantId} for this call.
  --project <id>            The project id, filling projectId for this call.
  --platform <id>           The platform id, filling {platformId} for this call.
  --json                    Write the response as JSON, and nothing else, to standard output.
  --fields <a,b.c>          Keep only these members, comma-separated, dotted for a nested one.
  --yes                     Confirm an irreversible operation without asking.
  --dry-run                 Check the request without applying it, on an operation that offers it.
  --idempotency-key <key>   The Idempotency-Key to send on a write; a fresh one when absent.
  --if-match <version>      The version to send as If-Match on a versioned update.
  --token <->               Read the bearer token from standard input: `--token -`.
  --client-id <id>          A service account's client id, with --client-secret.
  --client-secret <secret>  A service account's client secret, with --client-id.
  --verbose                 Show the request and the response headers on standard error.

Commands:
  list       Every VION support session across every tenant, whoever of the support staff opened it. Platform 
             principals have no push channel, so this is polled.
  start      Start a support session on a gateway, under a grant that already covers it. The session names 
             its own duration, capped by the support maximum and by what is left of the grant — the grant is 
             not one session's budget.
  terminate  End one's own support session early.

Commands:
  recreate  Migrate existing principals to RabbitMQ authorization tables. Drops and recreates the RabbitMQ 
            user records one principal at a time (no global wipe window) for all accepted tenant, integrator 
            and platform users and the tenant and integrator service accounts, then cleans up orphaned 
            RabbitMQ users - which are neither those principals nor edge gateways nor admins - and finally 
            recreates the edge gateway records.

---
## vion platform rabbit-mq-permission recreate
Description:
  Migrate existing principals to RabbitMQ authorization tables. Drops and recreates the RabbitMQ user records 
  one principal at a time (no global wipe window) for all accepted tenant, integrator and platform users and 
  the tenant and integrator service accounts, then cleans up orphaned RabbitMQ users - which are neither 
  those principals nor edge gateways nor admins - and finally recreates the edge gateway records.

Usage:
  vion platform rabbit-mq-permission recreate [options]

Options:
  -?, -h, --help            Show help and usage information
  --env <name>              The environment: production, test, or one defined with `context use`.
  --integrator <id>         The integrator id, filling {integratorId} for this call.
  --tenant <id>             The tenant id, filling {tenantId} for this call.
  --project <id>            The project id, filling projectId for this call.

  --dry-run                 Check the request without applying it, on an operation that offers it.
