Commands:
  create  The machine-identity form of the owner invitation beside this: no email user, no token, no accept 
          step — the redeem is the client_credentials grant. The created account does NOT appear in the 
          integrator's owners list, which enumerates invited users; it appears in the integrator's own Access 
          console, which is membership-driven.
  list    Get the service accounts that own the integrator. Never returns a secret.
  delete  A service account's identity and its membership are one row, so revoking ownership means destroying 
          the account — the honest mirror of removing an owner user.

---

  The machine-identity form of the owner invitation beside this: no email user, no token, no accept step — 
  the redeem is the client_credentials grant. The created account does NOT appear in the integrator's owners

       scopes are those where the caller or one of its groups holds an assignment of its own; a scope not 
       listed has the permissions of its nearest listed ancestor, or the root's. A `scopeId` that is not part 
       of the platform answers with no scope type and no permissions.

---
## vion platform my-permissions get
Description:
  Self-service: any authenticated platform member, user or service account. Without `scopeId`, the scopes are 
  those where the caller or one of its groups holds an assignment of its own; a scope not listed has the 
  permissions of its nearest listed ancestor, or the root's. A `scopeId` that is not part of the platform 
  answers with no scope type and no permissions.

Usage:
  vion platform my-permissions get [options]

Options:
  --scope-id <value>        The scopes to resolve, the parameter repeated once per scope; none for the 
                            caller's own.
  -?, -h, --help            Show help and usage information
  --env <name>              The environment: production, test, or one defined with `context use`.
  --integrator <id>         The integrator id, filling {integratorId} for this call.
  --tenant <id>             The tenant id, filling {tenantId} for this call.

             service account has an empty inbox. A page can hold fewer notifications than the limit while 
             more follow; the list ends where `nextCursor` is null.
  get        Reads one of the caller's notifications.
  mark-read  Repeatable: a notification already read keeps its time, and an id that is not the caller's 
             changes nothing.

## vion me notification list
Description:
  A notification of a context the caller is no longer a member of is not listed or counted. A service account 
  has an empty inbox. A page can hold fewer notifications than the limit while more follow; the list ends 
  where `nextCursor` is null.

Usage:
  vion me notification list [options]

Options:
  --limit <n>                         The largest number of notifications on the page, 30 when omitted. A 
                                      value above the maximum of 100 returns 100 notifications and is not an 
                                      error, and a value below 1 is.
  --cursor <value>                    The `nextCursor` of the previous page, or none for the first page.
  --unread                            Lists only the unread notifications when true.
  --auth-domain <value>               Lists only the notifications of one context, together with 
                                      authDomainRootScopeId.
  --auth-domain-root-scope-id <uuid>  Lists only the notifications of one context, together with authDomain.
  --locale <value>                    The language of the text, `de` or `en`. Without it, the caller's own 
                                      language, else English.
  -?, -h, --help                      Show help and usage information
  --env <name>                        The environment: production, test, or one defined with `context use`.
  --integrator <id>                   The integrator id, filling {integratorId} for this call.
  --tenant <id>                       The tenant id, filling {tenantId} for this call.
  --project <id>                      The project id, filling projectId for this call.
  --platform <id>                     The platform id, filling {platformId} for this call.
  --json                              Write the response as JSON, and nothing else, to standard output.
  --fields <a,b.c>                    Keep only these members, comma-separated, dotted for a nested one.
  --yes                               Confirm an irreversible operation without asking.
  --dry-run                           Check the request without applying it, on an operation that offers it.
  --idempotency-key <key>             The Idempotency-Key to send on a write; a fresh one when absent.
  --if-match <version>                The version to send as If-Match on a versioned update.
  --token <->                         Read the bearer token from standard input: `--token -`.
  --client-id <id>                    A service account's client id, with --client-secret.
  --client-secret <secret>            A service account's client secret, with --client-id.
  --verbose                           Show the request and the response headers on standard error.

## vion me invitation accept
Description:
  The token must carry an email address its identity provider verified, else the answer is 403. An invitation 
  sent to another address is not found, as is an id nobody has. An invitation already accepted is a conflict, 
  and an expired one is refused as invalid.

Usage:
  vion me invitation accept [options]

Options:
  --id <uuid>               The invitation's id, the id of the user membership it creates.
  -?, -h, --help            Show help and usage information
  --env <name>              The environment: production, test, or one defined with `context use`.

  --project <id>            The project id, filling projectId for this call.

  --fields <a,b.c>          Keep only these members, comma-separated, dotted for a nested one.

  --dry-run                 Check the request without applying it, on an operation that offers it.

## vion api
Description:
  Send a raw request to the environment's API with the resolved token, and render the answer as any command's.

Usage:
  vion api <method> <path> [options]

Arguments:
  <method>  GET, POST, PUT, DELETE or PATCH.
  <path>    The path under the environment's API, with its query if any: /tenant/<id>/edgeGateways

Options:
  --input <file|->             The JSON body from a file, or `-` for standard input.
  -f, --raw-field <key=value>  A body member as a string: key=value; a dotted key nests.
  -F, --field <key=value>      A body member typed as JSON: true, false, null, a number, @file for a file's 
                               content, else a string.

  --project <id>               The project id, filling projectId for this call.
  --platform <id>              The platform id, filling {platformId} for this call.
  --json                       Write the response as JSON, and nothing else, to standard output.
  --fields <a,b.c>             Keep only these members, comma-separated, dotted for a nested one.
  --yes                        Confirm an irreversible operation without asking.
  --dry-run                    Check the request without applying it, on an operation that offers it.
  --idempotency-key <key>      The Idempotency-Key to send on a write; a fresh one when absent.
  --if-match <version>         The version to send as If-Match on a versioned update.
  --token <->                  Read the bearer token from standard input: `--token -`.
  --client-id <id>             A service account's client id, with --client-secret.
  --client-secret <secret>     A service account's client secret, with --client-id.
  --verbose                    Show the request and the response headers on standard error.
